\section{Blueprint: the Ginv-only guarded reading}

\section{Verdict and audit boundary}

\textbf{GoalNeg2 remains OPEN.} Neither the cofinal noncancellation premise nor its negation was proved in round 12. This is a blueprint of verified conditional reductions, unconditional supporting facts, and the remaining mathematical inputs—not a completed guarded refutation.

Notation: \(A=G^{-1}_{\mathrm{all}}\), \(B=G^{-1}_{\mathrm{in}}\), \(D=\Delta^{(2)}_{\mathrm{in}}\), \(C=B-D\), and \(\Theta=A-B\). The intended failure is \(\operatorname{IsUnit}(A)\land\neg\operatorname{IsUnit}(A-D)\). The vacuum singularity used to construct the kernel is \textbf{not} itself the guarded failure point.

\section{Mathematical spine}

The pinned equivalence reduces the question to the eventual implication
\[
 A\text{ unit}\ \Longrightarrow\ A-D\text{ unit}.
\]
Localized well-definedness supplies the exact correction/pencil identity and the genuine localized inverses. In boundary coordinates it is the comparison of \(K_B=I+\Theta B^{-1}\) and \(K_C=I+\Theta C^{-1}\) on \(\operatorname{ran}\Theta\). Absolute smallness of \(D\) does not control \(K_B^{-1}\); a unit guard is not a uniform gap.

The qualitative refutation route chooses \(\ell=2\), \(a=1\), \(k=1\), \(q=2^j\), and an even \(R\ge18\) \textbf{before} the small-curvature cap. At a vacuum crossing, a first-order correction definite on the full kernel splits the corrected crossing from the carrier crossing. Negating the free interior profile handles either sign. The fully verified round-9 bridge then supplies arbitrarily small-curvature guarded failures with all class, geometry and positive-energy test obligations.

For a physical profile \(f\), define
\[
 z_u=C^2_0(W_0i_Eu),\qquad r_u(b)=\langle E_3,(i_EH_0z_u)(b)\rangle,
 \qquad F_f(u)=2\sum_{p\in\operatorname{plaqIn}\Omega_0}c_{r_u}(p)c_f(p).
\]
These are actual frozen operators, not reduced numerical matrices. The adaptive choice \(f=r_u\) gives curl-square energy. This detects one vector, not automatically an entire kernel. The exact common-profile condition is
\[
0\notin\overline{\operatorname{conv}}\{g(u):u\in\ker A_0(t_*),\ \|u\|=1\},
\qquad g(u)=2\,\operatorname{curl}_P^*\operatorname{curl}_P r_u.
\]
Round 12 proves that under the original whole-kernel charged-line condition this hull is the singleton \(\{g(u_0)/\|u_0\|^2\}\). Thus the remaining geometric route is full-kernel line containment plus actual crossing detection, at nested cofinal instances. Nonzero charged fields can have zero jets, and different nonzero unit responses can cancel; both shortcuts have compiled obstructions.

\section{Pinned quantifiers and guards}
The positive target is \texttt{P428Target8SG selAll selAll}, not a touching-selector target. Its exact order at \texttt{\detokenize{AHP/round3/guarded/pin/Statement.lean:113}} is
\[
\forall \ell\ge2,\ \forall a>0,\ \exists m_0\ge1,\ \forall m\ge m_0,\ \exists R_0,\ \forall R\ge R_0,
\quad \mathrm{SidePow6}(\ell,\ell^m,a)\ \land\ \exists a_0>0,\ \exists\gamma_0>0.
\]
This is followed by all depths \(k\ge1\), lattice extents and \(\alpha_0>0\) satisfying \(12\ell^m\alpha_0\le a_0\); then every TorusOK, Admissible, LambdaOK2 and ClassCoupled instance, with class constant 12 and \(\eta=(\ell^k)^{-1}\). The sole guard is invertibility of the uncorrected carrier; the conclusion is the coercive inequality for every test satisfying both support conditions. Corrected carrier and constraint inverses are not guarded.

The pencil target at \texttt{\detokenize{AHP/round3/guarded/pin/Statement.lean:133}} retains the scale/radius/cap order, adds a positive-energy competitor, and asks for invertibility of \(A-D\) under the carrier guard. The positive equivalence handles SidePow6 and coercivity constants without changing selectors or interchanging radius and cap. GoalNeg2 negates the entire positive headline, not one numerical instance.

\lean{AdalyFormal.Round2.Guarded.R2Prove.localCorrected}
\uses{}

theorem cofinal_in_class_curved_competitor :
    ∀ m₀ : ℕ, ∃ m : ℕ, m₀ ≤ m ∧ 1 ≤ m ∧
      ∀ R₀ : ℕ, ∃ R : ℕ, R₀ ≤ R ∧ 17*2 ≤ R*(2^m) ∧
        ∀ a₀ : ℝ, 0 < a₀ →
          ∃ (k : ℕ) (_ : 1 ≤ k) (Lu : PeriodicLatticeExtent) (α₀ : ℝ)
            (_ : 0 < α₀) (_ : (12 : ℝ)*(2^m : ℕ)*α₀ ≤ a₀)
            (_ : TorusOK 2 (2^m) Lu)
            (S : Tower (scaleUp 2 k Lu)) (_ : Admissible 2 (2^m) R k S)
            (Lam : Set (PeriodicLatticeSite Lu)) (_ : LambdaOK2 2 (2^m) R k S Lam)
            (U : LinkCfg (scaleUp 2 k Lu))
            (_ : ClassCoupled 2 (2^m) 12 k S α₀ ((2 : ℝ)^k)⁻¹ U)
            (Bt : Bond Lu → SuTwo),
            OnLambdaTildeIn Lam 2 Bt ∧ OnLambdaT Lam Bt ∧ 0 < bondEnergy Bt ∧
            (∃ e : Bond (scaleUp 2 k Lu), e.1 ∈ S.Om 0 ∧ U e ≠ 1) ∧
            (∃ p ∈ plaqIn (S.Om 0), periodicPlaquetteHolonomy U p ≠ 1) ∧
            (¬ ∃ g : PeriodicLatticeSite (scaleUp 2 k Lu) → G2,
              periodicGaugeTransform g U = fun _ => (1 : G2))
\end{verbatim}
\end{theorem}

theorem pencil_isUnit_of_weighted_boundary_gap (P : IngrBParams) (hell : 1 ≤ P.ell)

    (hB : ∀ x : ↥(bondSupp P.G),

\end{verbatim}
\end{theorem}

\begin{theorem}[bp-038]
\label{bp-038}
\lean{AdalyFormal.Round4.Guarded.GapR1.scalar_gap_obstruction}
\uses{}
\leanok
An exact scalar example has uniformly positive localized carriers and arbitrarily small correction, but an invertible all-carrier with singular corrected pencil. This is not a lattice counterexample.

\par Source: \texttt{\detokenize{AHP/round4/guarded/gap-r1/Check3.lean:550}}.
\par Status: B: builder-compiled only. Recorded exit 0.
\par Axiom record: \texttt{\detokenize{AHP/round4/guarded/gap-r1/Check3.lean.log:92}}.

\end{verbatim}
\par Direct blueprint inputs: none beyond the explicit hypotheses and frozen/Mathlib background.
\par Exact Lean signature:
\begin{verbatim}
theorem scalar_gap_obstruction (cap : ℝ) (hcap : 0 < cap) :
    ∃ (B D Θ : ℝ), B = 1 ∧ 0 < D ∧ |D| < cap ∧ 3 / 4 ≤ B - D ∧
      Θ ≠ 0 ∧ IsUnit B ∧ IsUnit (B - D) ∧ IsUnit (B + Θ) ∧
      ¬ IsUnit (B + Θ - D) ∧
      0 < |1 + Θ / B| ∧ |1 + Θ / B| < cap ∧ 1 + Θ / (B - D) = 0
\end{verbatim}
\end{theorem}

theorem dil_local_data_exterior_independent {q R : ℕ} (hq : 0 < q) (hR : 2 ∣ R)
    (hroom : 34 ≤ R * (2*q)) (ε a : ℝ)
    (V V' : LinkCfg (scaleUp 2 1 (dilLu q R))) :

      (dilU q R ε V') a (dilLam q R)) =
    dataS selIn selIn (gparams 2 1 (dilLu q R) (by norm_num) (dilTower q R)
      (dilU q R ε V) a (dilLam q R))
\end{verbatim}
\end{theorem}

\par Exact Lean signature:
\begin{verbatim}
theorem twoSite_exact_symmetry {q : ℕ} (hq : 0 < q) (R : ℕ) (ε t : ℝ)
    (μ : Fin 3) (hμ : μ ≠ 0) :
    ∃ T : ↥(bondSupp (geomOf 2 1 (dilTower q R))) ≃ₗᵢ[ℝ]
        ↥(bondSupp (geomOf 2 1 (dilTower q R))),
      (∀ v : ↥(bondSupp (geomOf 2 1 (dilTower q R))),
        ∀ e : Bond (scaleUp 2 1 (dilLu q R)),
        (T v : BondFnL2 _) e = (v : BondFnL2 _) (bondTranslation (twoSite q R μ) e)) ∧
      (∀ e : Bond (scaleUp 2 1 (dilLu q R)),
        twistU q R ε t (bondTranslation (twoSite q R μ) e) = twistU q R ε t e) ∧
      (∀ p : PeriodicLatticePlaquette (scaleUp 2 1 (dilLu q R)),
        periodicPlaquetteHolonomy (twistU q R ε t) (p.1 + twoSite q R μ, p.2) =
          periodicPlaquetteHolonomy (twistU q R ε t) p)
\end{verbatim}
\end{theorem}

\par Exact Lean signature:
\begin{verbatim}
theorem cofinal_curved_placement_no_separation :
    ∀ m₀ : ℕ, ∃ m : ℕ, m₀ ≤ m ∧ 1 ≤ m ∧
      ∀ R₀ : ℕ, ∃ R : ℕ, R₀ ≤ R ∧ 17*2 ≤ R*(2^m) ∧
        ∀ a₀ : ℝ, 0 < a₀ →
          ∃ (k : ℕ) (_ : 1 ≤ k) (Lu : PeriodicLatticeExtent) (α₀ : ℝ)
            (_ : 0 < α₀) (_ : (12 : ℝ)*(2^m : ℕ)*α₀ ≤ a₀)
            (_ : TorusOK 2 (2^m) Lu)
            (S : Tower (scaleUp 2 k Lu)) (_ : Admissible 2 (2^m) R k S)
            (Lam : Set (PeriodicLatticeSite Lu)) (_ : LambdaOK2 2 (2^m) R k S Lam)
            (U : LinkCfg (scaleUp 2 k Lu))
            (_ : ClassCoupled 2 (2^m) 12 k S α₀ ((2 : ℝ)^k)⁻¹ U)
            (Bt : Bond Lu → SuTwo),
            OnLambdaTildeIn Lam 2 Bt ∧ OnLambdaT Lam Bt ∧ 0 < bondEnergy Bt ∧
            (∃ e : Bond (scaleUp 2 k Lu), e.1 ∈ S.Om 0 ∧ U e ≠ 1) ∧
            (∃ p ∈ plaqIn (S.Om 0), periodicPlaquetteHolonomy U p ≠ 1) ∧
            (¬ ∃ g : PeriodicLatticeSite (scaleUp 2 k Lu) → G2,
              periodicGaugeTransform g U = fun _ => (1 : G2)) ∧
            ∀ a : ℝ, CPen 2 k Lu (by norm_num) S U a Lam ↔
              IsUnit (dataS selAll selAll (gparams 2 k Lu (by norm_num) S U a Lam)).Ginv
\end{verbatim}
\end{theorem}

theorem separation_of_sign_change
    (A : ℝ → E →L[ℝ] E) (D : E →L[ℝ] E) (hc : Continuous A)
    (hsA : ∀ t x y, ⟪A t x, y⟫ = ⟪x, A t y⟫)
    (hsD : ∀ x y, ⟪D x, y⟫ = ⟪x, D y⟫)
    (t₀ t₁ : ℝ) (h01 : t₀ ≤ t₁)
    (hguard : ∀ t, t₀ ≤ t → t ≤ t₁ → IsUnit (A t))
    (h₀ : PSD (A t₀ - D)) (w : E) (h₁ : ⟪w, (A t₁ - D) w⟫ < 0) :
    ∃ t : ℝ, t₀ ≤ t ∧ t < t₁ ∧ IsUnit (A t) ∧ ¬ IsUnit (A t - D)
\end{verbatim}
\end{theorem}

\par Exact Lean signature:
\begin{verbatim}
theorem pencil_rescaled_antitone (B Θ₀ Θ₁ : E →L[ℝ] E) (hB : PSD B) (hΘ : PSD Θ₀)
    {s t : ℝ} (hs0 : 0 < s) (hst : s ≤ t) (htπ : t ≤ Real.pi/2) (x : E) :
    ⟪x, (B + Real.cos t • Θ₀ + Real.sin t • Θ₁) x⟫ / Real.sin t ≤
      ⟪x, (B + Real.cos s • Θ₀ + Real.sin s • Θ₁) x⟫ / Real.sin s
\end{verbatim}
\end{theorem}

\begin{theorem}[bp-082]
\label{bp-082}
\lean{AdalyFormal.Round9.Guarded.AnalyticR1.schur_unit_iff}
\uses{}
\leanok
With the complementary block invertible, full operator invertibility is equivalent to injectivity of its Schur complement on the projected kernel space.

theorem schur_unit_iff {P Z : E →L[ℝ] E} (hP : P * P = P) (hC : IsUnit (schurC P Z)) :
    IsUnit Z ↔ ∀ x, P x = x → schurS P Z x = 0 → x = 0
\end{verbatim}
\end{theorem}

\par Exact Lean signature:
\begin{verbatim}
theorem schur_difference_tendsto {α : Type*} {l : Filter α} (P : E →L[ℝ] E)
    {Xf Df : α → E →L[ℝ] E} {c : α → ℝ} {X0 D1 : E →L[ℝ] E}
    (hXP : X0 * P = 0) (hPX : P * X0 = 0) (hC0 : IsUnit (schurC P X0))
    (hX : Tendsto Xf l (𝓝 X0)) (hD : Tendsto Df l (𝓝 0))
    (hcD : Tendsto (fun a => c a • Df a) l (𝓝 D1)) :
    Tendsto (fun a => c a • (schurS P (Xf a) - schurS P (Xf a - Df a)) - P * D1 * P) l (𝓝 0)
\end{verbatim}
\end{theorem}

\begin{verbatim}
theorem separation_of_vacuum_kernel
    (X : ℝ → ℝ → E →L[ℝ] E) (D : ℝ → E →L[ℝ] E)
    (hXs : ∀ ε t x y, ⟪X ε t x, y⟫ = ⟪x, X ε t y⟫)
    (hDs : ∀ ε x y, ⟪D ε x, y⟫ = ⟪x, D ε y⟫)
    (hXc : ∀ ε, Continuous (X ε))
    (t₀ η₀ : ℝ) (hη₀ : 0 < η₀)
    (hjoint : ∀ t, |t - t₀| < η₀ →
      Tendsto (fun p : ℝ × ℝ => X p.1 p.2) (𝓝[>] (0 : ℝ) ×ˢ 𝓝 t) (𝓝 (X 0 t)))
    (X' : E →L[ℝ] E) (hder : HasDerivAt (X 0) X' t₀)
    (D₁ : E →L[ℝ] E) (hD : Tendsto (fun ε : ℝ => ε⁻¹ • D ε) (𝓝[>] 0) (𝓝 D₁))
    (hne : ∃ v, X 0 t₀ v = 0 ∧ v ≠ 0)
    (hslope : ∀ v, X 0 t₀ v = 0 → v ≠ 0 → ⟪v, X' v⟫ < 0)
    (hsign : (∀ v, X 0 t₀ v = 0 → v ≠ 0 → ⟪v, D₁ v⟫ < 0) ∨
      (∀ v, X 0 t₀ v = 0 → v ≠ 0 → 0 < ⟪v, D₁ v⟫)) :
    ∃ δ : ℝ, 0 < δ ∧ ∀ ε : ℝ, 0 < ε → ε < δ →
      ∃ t : ℝ, IsUnit (X ε t) ∧ ¬ IsUnit (X ε t - D ε)
\end{verbatim}
\end{theorem}

    [NormedAddCommGroup F] [InnerProductSpace ℝ F] [FiniteDimensional ℝ F]
    (D : ℝ → BaseDataS E S F) (hL : ∀ ε, (D ε).Laws)
    (hH : ContinuousAt (fun ε => (D ε).H) 0)
    (hC2 : ∀ A, Tendsto (fun ε => (D ε).C2 A) (𝓝 0) (𝓝 ((D 0).C2 A)))
    (J' : E) (hJ : Tendsto (fun ε => ε⁻¹ • (D ε).J) (𝓝[≠] 0) (𝓝 J'))

\end{theorem}

theorem first_order_separation
    (A : ℝ → ℝ → E →L[ℝ] E) (D : ℝ → E →L[ℝ] E)
    (hsA : ∀ ε t x y, ⟪A ε t x, y⟫ = ⟪x, A ε t y⟫)
    (hsD : ∀ ε x y, ⟪D ε x, y⟫ = ⟪x, D ε y⟫)
    (hcA : ∀ ε, Continuous (A ε)) (ts : ℝ)
    (hjoint : ∃ η₀ : ℝ, 0 < η₀ ∧ ∀ t : ℝ, |t - ts| < η₀ → ∀ δ : ℝ, 0 < δ → ∃ r : ℝ, 0 < r ∧
      ∀ ε s : ℝ, |ε| < r → |s - t| < r → ‖A ε s - A 0 t‖ < δ)
    (hbefore : ∀ η : ℝ, 0 < η → ∃ t₀ : ℝ, ts - η < t₀ ∧ t₀ < ts ∧
      ∀ x : E, x ≠ 0 → 0 < ⟪x, A 0 t₀ x⟫)
    (hafter : ∀ η : ℝ, 0 < η → ∃ t₁ : ℝ, ts < t₁ ∧ t₁ < ts + η ∧ ∃ w : E, ⟪w, A 0 t₁ w⟫ < 0)
    (c : ℝ) (De : E →L[ℝ] E) (hc : 0 < c)
    (hDe : ∀ u : E, A 0 ts u = 0 → c * ‖u‖ ^ 2 ≤ ⟪u, De u⟫)
    (hDexp : ∀ μ : ℝ, 0 < μ → ∃ r : ℝ, 0 < r ∧ ∀ ε : ℝ, 0 < ε → ε < r →
      ‖D ε - ε • De‖ ≤ μ * ε) :
    ∃ ε₀ : ℝ, 0 < ε₀ ∧ ∀ ε : ℝ, 0 < ε → ε < ε₀ →
      ∃ t : ℝ, IsUnit (A ε t) ∧ ¬ IsUnit (A ε t - D ε)
\end{verbatim}
\end{theorem}

theorem scalar_on_complex_line (M J : E →L[ℝ] E) (hM : ∀ x y, ⟪M x, y⟫ = ⟪x, M y⟫)
    (hJ : ∀ x y, ⟪J x, J y⟫ = ⟪x, y⟫) (hMJ : ∀ x, M (J x) = J (M x))

\end{verbatim}

    (hs : ∀ t x y, ⟪A t x, y⟫ = ⟪x, A t y⟫) {t₀ t₁ : ℝ} (h01 : t₀ < t₁)
    (hpd : ∀ x, x ≠ 0 → 0 < ⟪x, A t₀ x⟫) (w : E) (hw : ⟪w, A t₁ w⟫ < 0)
    (hiso : ∀ t, ∃ η : ℝ, 0 < η ∧ ∀ s : ℝ, s ≠ t → |s - t| < η → IsUnit (A s)) :
    t₀ < psdEdge A t₀ t₁ ∧ psdEdge A t₀ t₁ < t₁ ∧
    PSD (A (psdEdge A t₀ t₁)) ∧ ¬ IsUnit (A (psdEdge A t₀ t₁)) ∧
    (∀ s, t₀ ≤ s → s ≤ psdEdge A t₀ t₁ → PSD (A s)) ∧

        ∃ v, ⟪v, A s v⟫ < 0)
\end{verbatim}
\end{theorem}

theorem vacuum_kernel_charge_split (q R : ℕ) (t : ℝ) (u : TwistSpace q R)
    (hu : vacA q R (uthPath q R) 1 t u = 0) :
    ∃ up um : TwistSpace q R, u = up + um ∧
      adEL (rotGauge (Real.pi / 2)) (geomOf 2 1 (dilTower q R)) up = up ∧
      adEL (rotGauge (Real.pi / 2)) (geomOf 2 1 (dilTower q R)) um = -um ∧
      ⟪up, um⟫ = 0 ∧ vacA q R (uthPath q R) 1 t up = 0 ∧ vacA q R (uthPath q R) 1 t um = 0
\end{verbatim}
\end{theorem}

    (factor δ σ : ℝ) (hf : 0 < factor) (hδ : 0 < δ) (hσ : σ = 1 ∨ σ = -1)
    (ha : a (n+1) = 0)
    (heq : ∀ j < n+1, 6*a j + Nonvanishing.left a j + a (j+1) =

theorem pair_completion (c s : ℝ) (hcs : c^2 + s^2 = 1) (y z : ℂ) :
    Complex.normSq y + Complex.normSq z + pairForm c s y z =
      c * (Complex.normSq y + Complex.normSq z) +

\end{verbatim}

theorem frozen_three_form
    (U : PeriodicLinkConfiguration (Matrix.specialUnitaryGroup (Fin 2) ℂ) L)
    (p : PeriodicLatticePlaquette L) (A : Bond L → SuTwo)
    (c s : ℝ) (x y z : ℂ)
    (hr : reHolonomy U p = realHol c) (hi : imHolonomy U p = imagHol s)
    (h1 : a1LM U p A = chargedMat x) (h2 : a2LM p A = 0)
    (h3 : a3LM p A = chargedMat y) (h4 : a4LM U p A = chargedMat z) :
    ⟪curlValueCovariant U A p, curlValueCovariant U A p⟫ +
      (1/2 : ℝ) * bilPlaq U p A A = tripleForm c s x y z
\end{verbatim}
\end{theorem}

\begin{verbatim}
theorem physical_kernel_line (G : TowerGeom L) (b : Bond L)
    (A : ↥(bondSupp G) →L[ℝ] ↥(bondSupp G))
    (hcomm : ∀ u, A (quarter G u) = quarter G (A u))
    (hp : ∀ u, bondTrace G b u = 0 → u ≠ 0 → 0 < ⟪u, A u⟫)
    (u₀ : ↥(bondSupp G)) (hu₀ : A u₀ = 0) (hne : u₀ ≠ 0) :
    quarter G (quarter G u₀) = -u₀ ∧
      ∀ u, A u = 0 → u ∈ Submodule.span ℝ {u₀, quarter G u₀}
\end{verbatim}
\end{theorem}

theorem star_negative_endpoint {ell M R k : ℕ} {Lu : PeriodicLatticeExtent}
    (hell0 : 0 < ell) (S : Tower (scaleUp ell k Lu))
    (Lam : Set (PeriodicLatticeSite Lu)) (a : ℝ)
    (hS : Admissible ell M R k S) (hell : 2 ≤ ell) (hroom : 17*ell ≤ R*M)
    {x : PeriodicLatticeSite (scaleUp ell k Lu)} (hbd : BdPt (S.Om 0) x)
    (ha : 0 ≤ a) (ha4 : a < 4) (X : SuTwo) (hX : X ≠ 0) :
    ∃ v : ↥(bondSupp (geomOf ell k S)),
      ⟪v, (dataS selAll selAll (gparams ell k Lu hell0 S
        (starPath (periodicLatticeShift x 0) Real.pi) a Lam)).Ginv v⟫ < 0
\end{verbatim}
\end{theorem}

\begin{verbatim}
theorem frozen_full_three_right
    (U : PeriodicLinkConfiguration (Matrix.specialUnitaryGroup (Fin 2) ℂ) L)
    (p : PeriodicLatticePlaquette L) (A : Bond L → SuTwo)
    (c s h k l : ℝ) (x y z : ℂ)
    (hr : reHolonomy U p = realHol c) (hi : imHolonomy U p = imagHol s)
    (h1 : a1LM U p A = lieMat h x) (h2 : a2LM p A = lieMat k y)
    (h3 : a3LM p A = lieMat l z) (h4 : a4LM U p A = 0) :
    ⟪curlValueCovariant U A p, curlValueCovariant U A p⟫ +
      (1/2 : ℝ) * bilPlaq U p A A = tripleForm c s x y z + c*(h+k+l)^2
\end{verbatim}
\end{theorem}

For nonnegative cosine, the two actual leaf masses plus the full local plaquette form dominate their cosine-weighted mass, retaining neutral terms.

\end{itemize}
\par Exact Lean signature:
\begin{verbatim}
theorem full_pair_mass_lower (c s h k l : ℝ) (y z : ℂ)
    (hc : 0 ≤ c) (hc1 : c ≤ 1) (hcs : c^2+s^2=1) :
    c*(Complex.normSq y+k^2 + (Complex.normSq z+l^2)) ≤
      Complex.normSq y+k^2 + (Complex.normSq z+l^2) +
        pairForm c s y z + c*(h+k+l)^2
\end{verbatim}
\end{theorem}

\end{itemize}
\par Exact Lean signature:
\begin{verbatim}
theorem full_star_mass_lower {ι : Type*} [Fintype ι]
    (c s rest : ℝ) (hc : 0 ≤ c) (hc1 : c ≤ 1) (hr : 0 ≤ rest)
    (hcs : c^2+s^2=1) (h k l : ι → ℝ) (y z : ι → ℂ) :
    c*(rest+∑ i, (Complex.normSq (y i)+(k i)^2 +
        (Complex.normSq (z i)+(l i)^2))) ≤
      rest+∑ i, (Complex.normSq (y i)+(k i)^2 +
        (Complex.normSq (z i)+(l i)^2) + pairForm c s (y i) (z i) +
          c*(h i+k i+l i)^2)
\end{verbatim}
\end{theorem}

theorem carrier_leaf_mass_le (G : TowerGeom L) (U : Cfg L) (a : ℝ) (ha : 0 ≤ a)
    (leaves : Finset (Bond L)) (hl : ∀ e ∈ leaves, e ∈ bondsLam G 0)
    (u : ↥(bondSupp G)) :

\end{verbatim}
\end{theorem}

    (leaves : Finset (Bond L)) (hl : ∀ e ∈ leaves, e ∈ bondsLam G 0)

theorem cofinal_hybrid_class_family :
    ∀ m₀ : ℕ, ∃ m : ℕ, m₀ ≤ m ∧ 1 ≤ m ∧
      ∃ q : ℕ, 0 < q ∧ 2^m = 2*q ∧
        ∀ R₀ : ℕ, ∃ R : ℕ, R₀ ≤ R ∧ 2 ∣ R ∧ 18 ≤ R ∧ 17*2 ≤ R*(2^m) ∧
          ∀ a₀ : ℝ, 0 < a₀ → ∃ α δ : ℝ,
            0 < α ∧ (12 : ℝ)*(2^m : ℕ)*α ≤ a₀ ∧ 0 < δ ∧ δ < 1 ∧
            TorusOK 2 (2^m) (dilLu q R) ∧ Admissible 2 (2^m) R 1 (dilTower q R) ∧
            LambdaOK2 2 (2^m) R 1 (dilTower q R) (dilLam q R) ∧
            ∃ Bt : Bond (dilLu q R) → SuTwo,

                ∃ p ∈ plaqIn ((dilTower q R).Om 0),
                  periodicPlaquetteHolonomy (hybridU q R δ ε V) p ≠ 1
\end{verbatim}
\end{theorem}

    (hsA : ∀ ε t x y, ⟪A ε t x, y⟫ = ⟪x, A ε t y⟫)
    (hsD : ∀ ε x y, ⟪D ε x, y⟫ = ⟪x, D ε y⟫)
    (ts : ℝ) (K : Submodule ℝ E) (g σ α c : ℝ) (At Ae De : E →L[ℝ] E)

\subsection{Alternative leaves retained, not silently assumed}

The older positive-kernel, exact two-mode/Schur, source-family, scalar recurrence/transfer and moving-root polynomial routes are conditional alternatives, not additional proved facts. Their exact assumptions are retained in the ledger's Lean signatures and direct source citations. The preferred adaptive route bypasses source-column and Bloch-normalization obligations, but does not prove those older dictionaries. No exact physical MovingRootCertificate, fixed transfer representation, or certified nonzero seed is supplied. There is \textbf{no numerical certificate} of any such representation. The positive alternatives need a class-uniform inverse-relative boundary-return bound or exact common-profile invisibility; localized coercivity and a small absolute correction do not give that bound.

\section{Numerical evidence attached to the open leaves — never proof}

All values below were checked against retained data/summary files, not recomputed. They are floating-point evaluations, not directed-rounding intervals, certified spectral enclosures, a Lean stencil/DFT identification or a proof of cofinality. Schur eigenvalues are \textbf{not} eigenvalues of the full carrier. In the right-star reconstruction the vector has unit nine-boundary-bond norm, not unit full-carrier norm; the physical normalization join remains unproved.

Without the tangent bond: \(5/4+3/4-2=0\). \textbf{AHP/round12/kernel/r2/TRIAL\_CHECKS.json}, tests produced by \textbf{trial\_crosscheck.py} in that directory. \mathrm{CofinalAnyPathCoupling}

\section{What the proposed failure point actually shows about inverses}

\textbf{There is still no unconditional cofinal failure family.} Conditional on O0 and the repaired integration, the failure point has the following inverse status:

\begin{itemize}
\item  Object  ---  Status at the conditional guarded failure 
\item  Uncorrected all-plaquette carrier \(A\)  ---  \textbf{A unit.} Its ordinary inverse \(G=A^{-1}\) exists, so the Ginv-only guard is satisfied. The qualitative/profile-reflection route can make this carrier positive definite. 
\item  Uncorrected all-plaquette constraint \(QGQ^*\), used in \(H\)  ---  \textbf{A unit in the admissible small-curvature bridge.} This follows from localized well-definedness and the transfer in Delta2\_lit\_eq\_loc, not from the carrier guard alone. 
\item  Localized base carrier \(B\), corrected localized carrier \(C=B-D\), and localized corrected constraint  ---  \textbf{Units} supplied by localized WellDefAt at the chosen small curvature. The uncorrected localized constraint is also a unit via NG\_unit\_of\_N1. Thus the lift forming D uses genuine inverses. 
\item  Corrected all-plaquette carrier, named G1invS  ---  \textbf{Not a unit:} it is \(A-D\). It has no ordinary inverse. 
\item  Corrected covariance \(G_1\)  ---  It is Ring.inverse of the nonunit above, hence \textbf{zero by totalization}, not an existing ordinary inverse. 
\item  Corrected all-plaquette constraint \(N_1=QG_1Q^*\) and corrected lift \(H_1\)  ---  \(N_1=0\); its totalized inverse and \(H_1\) vanish. The positive-energy competitor ensures the relevant coarse test space is nontrivial, so this zero constraint operator is not an ordinary invertible operator. 
\item  Auxiliary Dirichlet inverse for \(G'\)  ---  Genuine by the dilated Dirichlet-unit theorem. 
\item  Gauge Gram inverse for the projection  ---  Genuine at the flat point and in the sufficiently small curvature neighborhood used by regularity. 
\item  Scalar normalizations  ---  \(\ell=2\) and \(\eta>0\), so these are not singular scalar normalizations. 
\end{itemize}

The exact pencil identity is \textbf{AdalyFormal.D1Residual.G1inv\_lit\_eq\_pencil}, lean/src/DrRed1.lean:52. The totalized collapse is \textbf{AdalyFormal.D1Guarded.genFormS\_collapse\_G1inv}, lean/src/GdGap.lean:56:
\[
\operatorname{genFormS}(B_{\mathrm{test}})=-a\langle B_{\mathrm{test}},B_{\mathrm{test}}\rangle.
\]
Together with an admissible positive-energy test, that collapse defeats a positive coercive lower bound. It refutes the \textbf{carrier-only guarded, totalized formal reading}, if the remaining premise is proved. It is not a counterexample with every printed inverse existing, and it does not refute a version additionally guarded by full all-plaquette WellDefAt.
